{"forms":[{"form":"pitchforks","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["An agricultural hand tool comprising a fork with sparse, light tines, attached to a long handle, used for pitching hay (especially loose hay) high up onto a stack (as on a wagon or haystack, or into a haymow)."]},{"glosses":["A similar fork with slightly more and heavier tines, used for mucking stalls and pitching soiled bedding into a wagon or manure spreader."],"synonyms":[{"word":"dungfork"},{"word":"manure fork"},{"word":"graip"}]},{"glosses":["Any fork used for farm labor, even a digging fork (but such usage is often considered ignorant by experienced farmers)."],"tags":["broadly","informal"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The city council was facing the pitchforks once the public had realized what the new ordinance entailed.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["An angry mob; the power or effects of such a mob."],"tags":["figuratively","in-plural","metonymically"]},{"glosses":["A tuning fork."],"tags":["rare"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈpɪt͡ʃˌfɔɹk/"}],"upstream_line":242311,"upstream_offset":1202670176,"upstream_record_sha256":"08a07cd89c1dbeceecadf56f16feadf15db4fa1f451e1cc35eaaeee016360bd7","word":"pitchfork"}
